## Animation

### ActionRig

**Display name:** Action Rig
**Category:** `Animation`

Attach an editable component, skeleton, skinning, socket, collider, destruction-group, and animation contract to unchanged rest geometry for Three.js preview and glTF export.

#### Inputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `in` | Rest Geometry | `SpatialGeometry` |

#### Outputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `out` | Rigged Geometry | `SpatialGeometry` |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Range or options |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `rigJson` | `string` | `{"schemaVersion":2,"componentTree":[{"id":"root","parent":null,"pivot":[0,0,0],"tip":[0,1,0],"radius":1,"role":"structural","joint":true,"detachable":false}],"clips":[]}` | — |
| `skinMode` | `enum` | `geodesic` | — |
| `maxInfluences` | `integer` | `4` | min 1<br>max 4 |
| `falloff` | `number` | `4.0` | min 0.01<br>max 32.0 |
| `geodesicResolution` | `integer` | `40` | min 16<br>max 96 |
| `componentMode` | `enum` | `dominantBone` | — |
| `splitComponents` | `boolean` | `true` | — |
| `autoplay` | `string` | `""` | — |
| `playbackSpeed` | `number` | `1.0` | min -8.0<br>max 8.0 |

### PreserveGltfRig

**Display name:** Preserve Rigged GLB
**Category:** `Animation`

Load a self-contained rigged GLB through a preservation route. Web preview reads the original skin.joints, inverse bind matrices, JOINTS_0, WEIGHTS_0, node-index animation channels, and continuous skinned shell without semantic component splitting.

#### Inputs

None.

#### Outputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `out` | Rigged Geometry | `SpatialMesh` |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Range or options |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `string` | `""` | — |
| `projectRoot` | `string` | `""` | — |
| `scale` | `number` | `1.0` | min 1e-06 |
| `axisConversion` | `enum` | `none` | — |

### OrientedSdfSurface

**Display name:** Oriented SDF Surface
**Category:** `Mesh`

Reconstruct a new closed polygon surface from a baked oriented point cloud using a sparse moving-least-squares signed-distance field and Surface Nets. The payload contains no source faces or indices.

#### Inputs

None.

#### Outputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `out` | Surface | `SpatialGeometry` |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Range or options |

### OutlineSolid

**Display name:** Outline Solid
**Category:** `Mesh`

Welded solid from closed outline, columnsJson, or spine+edge stations. Select path with inputMode (no auto-detect). Prefer over micro-cylinder BooleanMesh chains for knife/plate bodies.

### ConditionOutline

**Display name:** Condition Outline
**Category:** `Spline`

Smooth then RDP-simplify SpatialSpline outlines. protectSpans are input-point index closed intervals (not Groups). Defaults are no-op. Active processing canonicalizes an explicit duplicate closing point on closed splines. Does not resample, repair self-intersections, or convert to columns.

### TaperedSweep

**Display name:** Tapered Sweep
**Category:** `Spline`

Sweep an elliptical section along an open spline with per-station radii and twist. Uses rotation-minimizing parallel-transport frames and collapses zero-radius endpoints to a true point.

#### Inputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `backbone` | Backbone | `SpatialSpline` |

#### Outputs

| ID | Label | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `out` | Mesh | `SpatialMesh` |

#### Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Range or options |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `stations` | `string` | `[{"u":0,"rx":1,"rz":1,"twist":0},{"u":0.65,"rx":0.6,"rz":0.45,"twist":0},{"u":1,"rx":0,"rz":0,"twist":0}]` | — |
| `radiusScale` | `number` | `1.0` | min 0.0001 |
| `radialSegments` | `integer` | `12` | min 3<br>max 128 |
| `sampleSpacing` | `number` | `1.0` | min 0.01 |
| `capStart` | `boolean` | `true` | — |
| `capEnd` | `boolean` | `true` | — |
| `upX` | `number` | `0` | — |
| `upY` | `number` | `1` | — |
| `upZ` | `number` | `0` | — |
| `shadeMode` | `enum` | `auto` | — |
| `cuspAngle` | `number` | `30.0` | min 0<br>max 180 |
